# Error Taxonomy for Prism

All Prism errors follow the code format `E-{CATEGORY}-{NNN}` and are surfaced as structured error responses (BC-2.10.007). Each error includes a severity level, retryability, and an actionable suggestion for the LLM agent.

## Severity Levels

| Severity | Definition | Example |
|----------|-----------|---------|
| **broken** | Feature completely unusable; requires human intervention or config change | Invalid credentials, config validation failure |
| **degraded** | Feature partially working; some data available but incomplete | Partial sensor failure in cross-client query, rate limiting |
| **cosmetic** | Minor issue; full functionality available with a workaround | Audit emission warning, unmapped OCSF field |

---

## AUTH: Authentication Errors

**Namespace note (v1.84 — E-AUTH-001/002/003 code adjudication):** The pre-v1.84 rows for E-AUTH-001/002/003 described OAuth2/bearer sensor-auth conditions ("OAuth2 token request failed", "OAuth2 token expired mid-request", "Bearer token rejected by {sensor}"). Investigation during S-5.02 HIGH-1 review found that those conditions are NEVER emitted with E-AUTH-001/002/003 in any code path. The live emitters are:
- E-AUTH-001: `PrismError::InvalidOrgSlug` (`prism-core`) AND `SpecEngineError::AuthAcquisitionFailed` (`prism-spec-engine`) — **LIVE COLLISION**
- E-AUTH-002: `PrismError::InvalidAnalystId` (`prism-core`) AND `SpecEngineError::AuthRefreshFailed` (`prism-spec-engine`) — **LIVE COLLISION**
- E-AUTH-003: `PrismError::InvalidClientId` (`prism-core`) ONLY — stale OAuth2/bearer taxonomy row, zero live emitters for that meaning

The OAuth2/bearer sensor-auth conditions described by the old E-AUTH-001/002/003 rows are real conditions but they ship under `SpecEngineError::AuthAcquisitionFailed` and `SpecEngineError::AuthRefreshFailed` whose displays already carry `"E-AUTH-001: auth token acquisition failed..."` and `"E-AUTH-002: auth refresh failed..."`. This means E-AUTH-001 and E-AUTH-002 are GENUINELY LIVE-COLLIDED: both `PrismError` (identity-validation) and `SpecEngineError` (sensor OAuth2) emit the same code for different conditions. **This is a blast-radius renumbering problem that CANNOT be fixed here.** See IMPLEMENTER FOLLOW-UP below.

The rows below are written to reflect the COMPLETE picture per ADR-035 canonical-row convention: each row's Description calls out both emitters and the collision status. E-AUTH-010/011 are added (ratifying the `PrismError` variants that S-5.02 pins via `ec_code_override`). E-AUTH-020 is added (ratifying `PrismError::Unauthorized`).

| Code | Severity | Category | Message Format | Retryable | Description |
|------|----------|----------|---------------|-----------|-------------|
| E-AUTH-001 | broken | authentication | **COLLIDED — two display formats:** (a) `"E-AUTH-001: invalid tenant ID: {reason}"` — `PrismError::InvalidOrgSlug` in `prism-core`; (b) `"E-AUTH-001: auth token acquisition failed for sensor '{sensor_id}', client '{client_id}': {detail}"` — `SpecEngineError::AuthAcquisitionFailed` in `prism-spec-engine` | No | **LIVE CODE COLLISION (v1.84 finding):** Two semantically distinct conditions share this code. (a) Identity validation: the org/tenant slug failed format validation (`PrismError::InvalidOrgSlug`). MCP maps to `-32602 INVALID_PARAMS` (caller-resolvable format error). Used by `prism-mcp` identity-validation arm and `prism-query` bc_gap_fill_tests. (b) Sensor OAuth2 token acquisition failed: the `AuthProvider::acquire_token` implementation for the sensor/client could not resolve credentials or complete the OAuth2 flow (`SpecEngineError::AuthAcquisitionFailed`). Used by `prism-spec-engine` pipeline executor and `prism-bin` spec-driven adapter. **Blast-radius renumbering required** — see IMPLEMENTER FOLLOW-UP note below the table. Neither side can be cheaply renumbered without sweeping: `prism-core/src/error.rs` display format, `prism-spec-engine/src/error.rs` display format, all `error_mapping.rs` arms, all tests pinning the code string, S-5.02 `ec_code_override` pins, BC-2.01.013/BC-2.01.016 citations. DO NOT ship new code that emits E-AUTH-001 for any third condition. |
| E-AUTH-002 | broken/degraded | authentication | **COLLIDED — two display formats:** (a) `"E-AUTH-002: invalid analyst ID: {reason}"` — `PrismError::InvalidAnalystId` in `prism-core`; (b) `"E-AUTH-002: auth refresh failed for sensor '{sensor_id}', client '{client_id}': HTTP 401 persisted after token re-acquisition on step '{step_name}'"` — `SpecEngineError::AuthRefreshFailed` in `prism-spec-engine` | No | **LIVE CODE COLLISION (v1.84 finding):** Two semantically distinct conditions share this code. (a) Identity validation: the analyst identifier failed format validation (`PrismError::InvalidAnalystId`). MCP maps to `-32602 INVALID_PARAMS` (caller-resolvable format error). Severity: broken. (b) Sensor OAuth2 auth refresh failed: double-401 after token re-acquisition — pipeline aborts; no further retries (`SpecEngineError::AuthRefreshFailed`). This variant is returned only for refreshable auth types (e.g., `Oauth2ClientCredentials`). Used by `prism-spec-engine` and `prism-bin`; pinned by `bc_2_01_013_spec_driven_adapter.rs` test `F-004-R` which asserts the display starts with `"E-AUTH-002"`. Severity: degraded. **Blast-radius renumbering required** — see IMPLEMENTER FOLLOW-UP note below the table. DO NOT ship new code that emits E-AUTH-002 for any third condition. |
| E-AUTH-003 | broken | authentication | `"E-AUTH-003: invalid client ID: {reason}"` | No | Client identifier failed format validation. Emitted by `PrismError::InvalidClientId` in `prism-core`. MCP maps to `-32602 INVALID_PARAMS` (caller-resolvable format error). Used by `prism-mcp` identity-validation arm (`error_mapping.rs` line 164), `prism-query` bc_gap_fill_tests, and S-5.02 `tool_dispatch_tests`. **v1.84 note:** the pre-v1.84 taxonomy described E-AUTH-003 as "Bearer token rejected by {sensor}" (an OAuth2/bearer sensor-auth condition). That condition has zero live emitters with E-AUTH-003 code — the row was stale spec-only prose. E-AUTH-003 is ratified to the single live emitter: `PrismError::InvalidClientId`. No collision on this code. |
| E-AUTH-004 | broken | authentication | "Cookie authentication failed for {sensor} on client '{client_id}'" | No | Cyberint cookie-based auth flow failed |
| E-AUTH-005 | broken | authentication | "Credentials not found for ({client_id}, {sensor_id})" | No | No credentials in keyring or file backend for the specified sensor |
| E-AUTH-006 | broken | authentication | "Empty or invalid API key for cookie_roundtrip sensor '{sensor}' on client '{client_id}'" | No | API key resolved but is empty, whitespace-only, exceeds maximum cookie value length (4096 bytes), or contains RFC 6265-illegal cookie characters (e.g., semicolons, control characters). Fail-fast at acquire_token time; no HTTP request attempted. Enforced by StaticCookieAuthProvider per BC-2.01.017. |
| E-AUTH-007 | broken | authentication | "Credential resolver backend unavailable for ({client_id}, {sensor_id}): {detail}" | Yes | Credential backend infrastructure failed — file read error (file exists but is unreadable, I/O error), keyring service unavailable (daemon not running, DBus error), or env _FILE path resolution raised a filesystem error. Distinct from E-AUTH-005 ("credential not found"): E-AUTH-005 means the backend is healthy but no entry exists; E-AUTH-007 means the backend itself is down or unreachable. Retryable because the underlying infrastructure condition (service restart, file permission fix) may resolve. Enforced by StaticCookieAuthProvider per BC-2.01.017 EC-017-010. Maps to `CredentialResolutionError::BackendUnavailable` in `prism_credentials::resolution`. |
| E-AUTH-010 | broken | authentication | `"E-AUTH-010: auth token expired"` | No | The authenticated credential (bearer token, session token) has expired and must be renewed. Emitted by `PrismError::AuthTokenExpired` in `prism-core/src/error.rs` (`#[error("E-AUTH-010: auth token expired")]`). Unit variant — no fields. MCP routes to `-32000 INTERNAL_ERROR` with redacted "Internal error; see audit log" message (authentication infrastructure failure, not caller-param issue). S-5.02 `error_mapping.rs` pins this code via `ec_code_override: Some("E-AUTH-010")` on the `AuthTokenExpired` arm of `prism_error_to_structured_call_result`, because `map_prism_error` returns `INTERNAL_ERROR/"Internal error; see audit log"` for this variant (no `E-` prefix in the message to infer). BC-2.10.007 structured error: `category: "authentication"`, `original_params_valid: true` (the params were well-formed; the credential itself failed), `retryable: false` (agent must re-authenticate). **v1.84: ratifying the code that `PrismError::AuthTokenExpired` already emits and that S-5.02 HIGH-1 fix pins.** |
| E-AUTH-011 | broken | authentication | `"E-AUTH-011: auth token invalid: {reason}"` | No | The authenticated credential is structurally invalid or has been revoked. Emitted by `PrismError::AuthTokenInvalid { reason: String }` in `prism-core/src/error.rs` (`#[error("E-AUTH-011: auth token invalid: {reason}")]`). The `{reason}` field describes the specific invalidity (e.g., "signature verification failed", "token format unrecognized"). MCP routes to `-32000 INTERNAL_ERROR` with redacted "Internal error; see audit log" message (authentication infrastructure failure). S-5.02 `error_mapping.rs` pins this code via `ec_code_override: Some("E-AUTH-011")` on the `AuthTokenInvalid` arm of `prism_error_to_structured_call_result`, same rationale as E-AUTH-010. BC-2.10.007 structured error: `category: "authentication"`, `original_params_valid: true`, `retryable: false`. **v1.84: ratifying the code that `PrismError::AuthTokenInvalid` already emits and that S-5.02 HIGH-1 fix pins.** |
| E-AUTH-020 | broken | authentication | `"E-AUTH-020: unauthorized: {action}"` | No | The caller lacks the required permission for the requested action. Emitted by `PrismError::Unauthorized { action: String }` in `prism-core/src/error.rs` (`#[error("E-AUTH-020: unauthorized: {action}")]`). The `{action}` field names the denied operation (e.g., "read audit log", "invoke write tool"). MCP routes to `-32002 FORBIDDEN` (per `error_mapping.rs` line 58: `// E-AUTH-020: Unauthorized → -32002 Forbidden`). BC-2.10.007 structured error: `category: "authentication"`, `original_params_valid: false` (inferred from INVALID_PARAMS-class semantics), `retryable: false`. **v1.84: ratifying the code that `PrismError::Unauthorized` already emits.** Verified in `prism-core/tests/ac_5_prism_error_display.rs` via `assert_error_code_prefix(&err, "E-AUTH-020")`. |

**IMPLEMENTER FOLLOW-UP — E-AUTH-001/002 collision (HIGH severity, requires separate maintenance story):**

E-AUTH-001 is emitted by two variants in two crates for two unrelated conditions:
- `PrismError::InvalidOrgSlug` in `prism-core` → `"E-AUTH-001: invalid tenant ID: {reason}"`
- `SpecEngineError::AuthAcquisitionFailed` in `prism-spec-engine` → `"E-AUTH-001: auth token acquisition failed for sensor '{sensor_id}', client '{client_id}': {detail}"`

E-AUTH-002 is emitted by two variants in two crates for two unrelated conditions:
- `PrismError::InvalidAnalystId` in `prism-core` → `"E-AUTH-002: invalid analyst ID: {reason}"`
- `SpecEngineError::AuthRefreshFailed` in `prism-spec-engine` → `"E-AUTH-002: auth refresh failed for sensor '{sensor_id}', client '{client_id}': HTTP 401 persisted..."`

**Blast radius of renumbering either side:**
- `prism-core/src/error.rs` — `#[error(...)]` format string for the affected variant
- `prism-spec-engine/src/error.rs` — `#[error(...)]` format string for the affected variant
- `prism-mcp/src/error_mapping.rs` — comment headers, arm code (for prism-core side)
- `prism-bin/src/spec_driven_adapter.rs` — doc comments citing E-AUTH-002
- `prism-bin/tests/bc_2_01_013_spec_driven_adapter.rs` — ~12 test assertions pinning `"E-AUTH-002"`
- `prism-spec-engine/tests/crowdstrike_oauth2_plugin_tests.rs` — assertions pinning `"E-AUTH-002"`
- BC-2.01.013 §Error Cases (error-taxonomy reference)
- BC-2.01.016 §Error Cases (comment in `prism-spec-engine/src/error.rs` line 325)
- `prism-core/tests/ac_5_prism_error_display.rs` — `assert_error_code_prefix` tests

**Proposed renumber (for orchestrator routing to implementer):** The identity-validation meanings (`PrismError::InvalidOrgSlug/InvalidAnalystId`) are more widely referenced in MCP tooling tests and have shorter blast radius. The sensor-auth meanings (`SpecEngineError::AuthAcquisitionFailed/AuthRefreshFailed`) have fewer external references. Recommended: reallocate the sensor-auth variants to new codes at the E-AUTH tail — e.g., `AuthAcquisitionFailed → E-AUTH-008`, `AuthRefreshFailed → E-AUTH-009` (verify both are free before allocating; this taxonomy currently shows E-AUTH-004..007 allocated, so E-AUTH-008/009 appear free). **This taxonomy does NOT pre-allocate E-AUTH-008/009 — the implementer must confirm they are free and allocate them in the same burst as the code fix.**

## SENSOR: Sensor API Errors

| Code | Severity | Category | Message Format | Retryable | Description |
|------|----------|----------|---------------|-----------|-------------|
| E-SENSOR-001 | degraded | upstream_error | "E-SENSOR-001: sensor {sensor} returned HTTP {status}: {body}" | Yes | Sensor adapter received an unexpected HTTP status from the upstream API. Emitted by `PrismError::SensorHttpError { sensor, status, body }`. Covers any non-200/non-429 HTTP response (4xx client errors, 5xx server errors). The `{body}` is placed in `upstream_message` only per DI-006 — never interpolated into prose `content[].text`. MCP maps to `-32000 INTERNAL_ERROR` (sensor infrastructure fault; detail in audit log). |
| E-SENSOR-002 | degraded | upstream_error | "E-SENSOR-002: sensor {sensor} timed out after {elapsed_ms}ms" | Yes | Sensor adapter timed out waiting for a response from the upstream API. Emitted by `PrismError::SensorTimeout { sensor, elapsed_ms }`. The elapsed time in milliseconds is diagnostic-only and appears in the audit log; it is NOT surfaced in the MCP response prose. MCP maps to `-32000 INTERNAL_ERROR`. |
| E-SENSOR-003 | degraded | upstream_error | "E-SENSOR-003: sensor {sensor} response parse error: {detail}" | No | Sensor adapter received a response that could not be parsed as the expected format (malformed JSON, missing structural fields, type mismatch). Emitted by `PrismError::SensorResponseParse { sensor, detail }`. The `{detail}` is placed in `upstream_message` only per DI-006. Non-retryable: parse errors on a fixed endpoint indicate a structural API contract change that requires operator attention. MCP maps to `-32000 INTERNAL_ERROR`. |
| E-SENSOR-004 | degraded | upstream_error | "{sensor} API returned unexpected response format" | Yes | HTTP 200 but body is not valid JSON or missing structural fields |
| E-SENSOR-005 | degraded | upstream_error | "Partial results from {sensor}: query truncated after page {n}" | No | Sensor became unavailable mid-pagination; partial results returned |
| E-SENSOR-020 | degraded | transient | "E-SENSOR-020: sensor {sensor} rate limited; retry after {retry_after_ms}ms" | Yes | Sensor API returned HTTP 429 Too Many Requests. Emitted by `PrismError::SensorRateLimited { sensor, retry_after_ms }`. The `retry_after_ms` field is a required `u64` (never `Option`) — the rate-limit error always carries a retry delay extracted from the upstream `Retry-After` header (or a sensible default if the header is absent). In BC-2.10.007 structured error responses: `retry_after_seconds` = `retry_after_ms / 1000` (always a non-null `u64` for this code, never `null` — distinct from all other SENSOR codes where `retry_after_seconds` is `null`); `category` = `"transient"`; `retryable` = `true`. MCP maps to `-32000 INTERNAL_ERROR` (rate-limit state is sensor infrastructure; detail in audit log). Ratified v1.83: S-5.02 implementation wires `ec_code_override: Some("E-SENSOR-020")` and BC-2.10.007 v1.6 specifies `to_error_data_with_retry` to thread `retry_after_ms` through the structured error builder. |

## OCSF: Normalization Errors

| Code | Severity | Category | Message Format | Retryable | Description |
|------|----------|----------|---------------|-----------|-------------|
| E-OCSF-001 | broken | configuration | "OCSF protobuf descriptor failed to load: {error}" | No | Fatal startup error; build-time issue |
| E-OCSF-002 | cosmetic | upstream_error | "OCSF field '{field}' unmappable from {sensor} record type '{type}'" | No | Field preserved in raw_extensions; OCSF message still valid |
| E-OCSF-003 | cosmetic | upstream_error | "Timestamp parse failed for {sensor} record; using fetch timestamp as fallback" | No | Record included with degraded timestamp accuracy |
| E-OCSF-004 | degraded | upstream_error | "OCSF protobuf encoding failed for record from {sensor}" | No | Record skipped; logged at error level |
| E-OCSF-024 | degraded | upstream_error | "E-OCSF-024: timestamp parse failed for field={field}: raw value={raw}" | No | Record-level datetime normalization failure. Emitted by `SpecDrivenMapper::parse_timestamp_to_epoch_ms` (`prism-ocsf`) when a `ColumnType::Datetime` column value fails all supported parse attempts (RFC-3339, ISO 8601 without timezone, Unix integer seconds). `{field}` = column name; `{raw}` = raw string value from sensor response (operator audit log only — not exposed to callers). MCP routes to `-32000 INTERNAL_ERROR` ("Internal error; see audit log"); raw field data never reaches the caller surface (AD-017 operator boundary). The specific record is skipped; the query continues and returns remaining records. BC-2.02.004 AC-4, S-1.05 Edge Cases. Pre-existing variant introduced in S-1.05; also exercised by S-PRISMQL-NATIVE-TEMPORAL-TYPING-001 datetime normalization pipeline. |

## CRED: Credential Errors

Canonical E-CRED-001..010 namespace per ADR-035 (E-CRED Namespace Reconciliation, 2026-06-07). Every code maps to exactly one semantic condition. Supersedes the prior 5-row table (v1.61) which had a code collision at E-CRED-005 and undeclared codes (E-CRED-009, E-CRED-010). See DRIFT-ECRED-TAXONOMY-001 and S-MAINT-ECRED-TAXONOMY-SYNC-001 for migration context.

| Code | Severity | Category | Message Format | Retryable | Description |
|------|----------|----------|---------------|-----------|-------------|
| E-CRED-001 | broken | validation | `"E-CRED-001: invalid credential name '{name}': {reason}"` | No | Credential name fails validation — empty, contains path-traversal characters (`..`, `/`, `\`), or matches a forbidden pattern. `{reason}` carries the sub-condition (e.g., "name is empty", "path-traversal character '/' in name", "forbidden pattern"). Subsumed the prior taxonomy E-CRED-004 (path-traversal-only) and aligns with `PrismError::InvalidCredentialName` in `prism_core::error`. |
| E-CRED-002 | broken | configuration | `"E-CRED-002: credential not found: {name}"` | No | Credential is not configured in any tier of the resolution chain (Tier 1 file var, Tier 2 env var, Tier 3 keyring, Tier 4 CRUD store all exhausted). Maps to `PrismError::CredentialNotFound`. |
| E-CRED-003 | broken | security | `"E-CRED-003: credential access denied for {name} — credential values never transit AI context"` | No | Credential value access blocked at the AI-opaque safety boundary (AD-017). The MCP layer maps this to JSON-RPC `-32002 Forbidden`. Maps to `PrismError::CredentialAccessDenied`. |
| E-CRED-004 | broken | infrastructure | `"E-CRED-004: credential store error (backend={backend}): {reason}"` | No | Backend-level credential store operation failed (RocksDB lock, keyring write failure, deserialization error). `{backend}` identifies the backend type (e.g., `rocksdb`, `keyring`). Maps to `PrismError::CredentialStoreError`. |
| E-CRED-005 | broken | configuration | `"E-CRED-005: credential file I/O error for '{path}': {reason}"` | No | Tier-1 file-backed credential: the file is missing, the path points to a directory instead of a file, or the file exists but cannot be read. `{reason}` carries the sub-condition (e.g., "file does not exist", "path is a directory, not a regular file", "read failed: {io_err}"). Emitted inline in the `reason` field of `PrismError::InvalidCredentialName` by `prism_credentials::resolve_secret` (formerly mislabeled E-CRED-009 in v1.61 and earlier). Note: the correct long-term design is a dedicated `PrismError::CredentialFileIo` variant; string-literal renumber is the immediate fix per ADR-035 §Blast-Radius. |
| E-CRED-006 | broken | authentication | `"E-CRED-006: credential encryption error: {reason}"` | No | AES-GCM or equivalent cipher failure on the encrypted-file backend (encryption on write or decryption on read). Maps to `PrismError::CredentialEncryptionError` (renumbered from E-CRED-005 per ADR-035 §D2). |
| E-CRED-007 | broken | configuration | `"E-CRED-007: encryption key not configured: {reason}"` | No | The encryption passphrase or key environment variable is not set for the encrypted-file backend. This is the prerequisite failure for E-CRED-006 — you cannot encrypt or decrypt without the key. Maps to `PrismError::EncryptionKeyMissing` (renumbered from E-CRED-006 per ADR-035 §D2). |
| E-CRED-008 | broken | infrastructure | `"E-CRED-008: OS keyring unavailable: {reason}"` | No | OS keyring service is inaccessible at any credential resolution tier (macOS Keychain locked, Linux libsecret D-Bus unavailable, `NoStorageAccess`, `NoKeyringService`, `spawn_blocking` panic). Hard error per ADR-034 §D4: SOUL.md §4 prohibits silent fall-through when the backend itself is unavailable. The `{reason}` field is a system error message from the keyring backend — it NEVER contains a credential value (AD-017). Operator remedy: unlock keyring / restart D-Bus, or use Tier 1/2 env vars (`PRISM_CLIENTS_{ID}_SENSORS_{SENSOR}_{REF}`). Maps to `CredentialResolutionError::BackendUnavailable` in `prism_credentials::resolution`. FORWARD-RESERVED: the only emitter of E-CRED-008 is the S-DEMO-003 Tier-3 keyring path (`CredentialResolutionError::BackendUnavailable`), which is pending merge on `feature/S-DEMO-003`. No develop-branch code emits E-CRED-008 yet — this is intentional, documented forward-definition per ADR-035, not drift. Consolidates taxonomy E-CRED-001 (generic keyring unavailable, spec-only prior to v1.61), taxonomy E-CRED-005 (Tier-3 keyring unavailable, per ADR-034 §D4 — amended by ADR-035 §D5), and `PrismError::KeyringError` E-CRED-010 (zero production callers, variant retired per ADR-035 §D4). |
| E-CRED-009 | broken | authentication | `"E-CRED-009: credential decryption failed for ({client_id}, {sensor_id}): {reason}"` | No | Credential data decryption failed — key material has changed or the encrypted file is corrupted. `{client_id}` and `{sensor_id}` provide operator context for identifying the affected credential slot. FORWARD-RESERVED: no develop-branch `PrismError` variant implements this code yet (the prior taxonomy declared it as E-CRED-003; it was never implemented in code). The code is declared here to reserve the slot and define the canonical display format per ADR-035 §Rationale for E-CRED-009. |
| E-CRED-010 | — | — | RESERVED — do not allocate | — | Reserved. E-CRED-010 was the number of the retired `PrismError::KeyringError` variant (zero production callers, removed per ADR-035 §D4). Reserving rather than recycling prevents confusion for any historical log entries that may contain `"E-CRED-010: keyring error:"`. Future namespace extension uses E-CRED-011 onward. |

## FLAG: Feature Flag Errors

| Code | Severity | Category | Message Format | Retryable | Description |
|------|----------|----------|---------------|-----------|-------------|
| E-FLAG-001 | broken | permission | "Not enabled in client config: capability '{capability}' is not in the runtime configuration for client '{client_id}'" | No | Runtime capability tier denied — the capability path is not granted `Allow` under `[clients.{client_id}.capabilities]` in the runtime TOML. Surfaced as `PrismError::CapabilityDenied` ("CAPABILITY_DENIED: capability '{capability}' denied for client '{client_id}': {reason}", BC-2.04.015) via `CapabilityCheckResult::DeniedRuntime`; the structured `suggestion` field carries the exact TOML remediation path + restart instruction. Message Format is the verbatim shipped `reason` (prism-security `feature_flag.rs` `DeniedRuntime` arm; ADR-035 canonical-row convention) — regularized at v1.72 (MCP cascade P4-04) from the legacy "Write capability '{path}' not enabled for client '{client_id}'" shape, which matched neither the shipped reason nor BC-2.04.015's pinned "Not enabled in client config" reason family (code and BC agree; the taxonomy row was the outlier). Sibling compile-tier denial: E-FLAG-002 (registry-derived, same `CapabilityDenied` surfacing). |
| E-FLAG-002 | broken | permission | "Write capability '{path}' denied: no write-endpoint declaration (no [[write_endpoints]] entry in the sensor's TOML spec)" | No | Compile-time capability tier denied — registry-derived per BC-2.16.012. Sensor-write dispatch is registry-driven: `write_pipeline.rs` derives the compile gate from `WriteEndpointRegistry` presence (a sensor has write capability if and only if its TOML spec declares a matching `[[write_endpoints]]` section loaded at boot). The `{sensor}-write` Cargo features are empty test-gating declarations in `prism-query` (preserve test coverage under `--all-features`), NOT the gate. Surfaced as `PrismError::CapabilityDenied` (CAPABILITY_DENIED display, BC-2.04.015) via `CapabilityCheckResult::DeniedCompileTime`. Surviving genuinely feature-gated case: the alias-write path (`alias.write`, BC-2.11.008) — `alias_write_compile_gate()` derives the compile gate from `#[cfg(feature = "alias-write")]` and produces the same `DeniedCompileTime` denial class. |
| E-FLAG-003 | broken | permission | "Token expired for action '{action_summary}'" | No | Confirmation token TTL exceeded |
| E-FLAG-004 | broken | permission | "Token already consumed for action '{action_summary}'" | No | Single-use token reuse attempt |
| E-FLAG-005 | broken | permission | "Token action hash mismatch" | No | Confirmed action differs from original request |
| E-FLAG-006 | broken | permission | "Write operation with client_id: null not supported" | No | Write operations require an explicit client_id; cross-client writes are not permitted |
| E-FLAG-007 | degraded | permission | "Token store capacity reached (100 active tokens)" | Yes | Hard cap on active confirmation tokens; retry after existing tokens expire (up to 300s). Confirm or cancel pending actions to free capacity sooner. |
| E-FLAG-008 | broken | permission | "Confirmation token not found: '{token_id}'" | No | Token does not exist in the in-memory store (server may have restarted, or token_id is invalid). Agent must re-request via the original write tool. |

## STATE: Pagination State Errors

| Code | Severity | Category | Message Format | Retryable | Description |
|------|----------|----------|---------------|-----------|-------------|
| E-STATE-001 | degraded | transient | "Pagination cursor invalid or expired for {sensor}/{source}" | No | Ephemeral cursor not found in-memory (server restarted, expired, or corrupted). Start a new query. |
| E-STATE-002 | degraded | transient | "Active cursor cap ({cap}) reached for {sensor}/{source}" | Yes | Maximum active pagination cursors reached after lazy cleanup of expired cursors. Complete or abandon existing pagination sessions before starting new ones. Retryable because cursors expire (600s TTL). |

## CACHE: Response Cache Errors

| Code | Severity | Category | Message Format | Retryable | Description |
|------|----------|----------|---------------|-----------|-------------|
| E-CACHE-001 | broken | transient | "Cache invalidation failed during write for ({client_id}, {sensor_id}, {source_id}): {reason}" | No | Cache invalidation failed during a write operation. The write itself succeeded at the sensor, but the cache may contain stale data. Cache synchronization failure triggers process exit with exit code 2 (per interface-definitions.md exit codes). Non-fatal failures (e.g., serialization) are logged but do not terminate. **Scope clarification (QRY-P8-01 adjudication, v1.74):** This taxonomy row describes the write-path cache-invalidation failure surface (caller-visible message template above). The `partition_counts` Mutex poison condition in `prism-query/src/cache.rs` (`lock_partition_counts`) is a DISTINCT internal condition — it propagates as `PrismError::Internal { detail: "cache mutex poisoned — internal state may be inconsistent; terminate and restart the query engine" }` (prose-only detail, no embedded E- code prefix per the `Internal.detail` convention below). That condition is NOT described by this row; it is an unrecoverable internal invariant violation that routes to `-32000 INTERNAL_ERROR` and requires process restart. No separate taxonomy code is required for the mutex-poison condition because it is unreachable by callers (operator-facing only). |

## CFG: Configuration Errors

### CFG-001..014: Customer Config Startup Validation (Wave 3 / BC-3.3.004)

> **SECTION RETIRED per ADR-037 (2026-06-10).** The `prism-customer-config` crate that emitted every code in this table (E-CFG-001..019) is retired with the ADR-010 `customers/{org_slug}.toml` schema (ADR-037 supersedes ADR-010; crate deletion executed by review-cycle Fix PR 3). No live code path emits these codes' customer-config semantics. All IDs are preserved per append_only_numbering (DF-030) and are never reused. Rule tables are preserved in the retired BC bodies: R-CUST-001..016 in BC-3.3.004; E-CFG-017..019 rules in BC-3.3.001 (E-CFG-018 also in BC-3.3.004). Per-row annotations below name the successor surface or code where the validation intent survives.
>
> **Namespace-collision drift note (surfaced 2026-06-10; ADJUDICATED per ADR-038, 2026-06-10):** live `prism_core::PrismError` runtime codes renumbered to E-CFG-100..106 (see the CFG-100..106 runtime section below); retired E-CFG-000..031 numbers permanently tombstoned, never reusable (POL-1 append_only_numbering / DF-030); future runtime codes allocate E-CFG-107 onward.

These codes were emitted during the `customers/*.toml` startup validation pass (BC-3.3.004, ADR-010 §2.3) implemented by the retired `prism-customer-config` crate. Each message included the filename, error code, and a human-readable description naming the offending value.

| Code | Severity | Category | Message Format | Retryable | Description |

Successor surface: `prism.toml` `[[orgs]]` required-field validation per BC-2.21.001 (boot step 3; exit 2; collect-all stderr report, no E- code). NOTE: Collision resolved per ADR-038 — live code ("config file not found") renumbered to E-CFG-103.** Missing required top-level field (`org_id`, `org_slug`, `display_name`), or a required string field that is present but empty (e.g., `display_name = ""`). Does NOT cover `schema_version` (handled by E-CFG-030/E-CFG-031 per BC-3.3.003). R-CUST-001 / D-052. |
| E-CFG-002 | broken | configuration | "customers/{file}: E-CFG-002: org_slug '{slug}' does not match filename stem '{stem}'" | No | **RETIRED per ADR-037 (2026-06-10); rule R-CUST-002 preserved in BC-3.3.004 body. `org_slug` no longer derives from a filename (declared in `prism.toml` `[[orgs]]` per BC-2.21.001); nearest successor analogues for path↔identity matching on the ADR-029 overlay surface: E-SPEC-020 (instance_id vs path) and E-SPEC-022 (directory slug vs OrgRegistry). NOTE: Collision resolved per ADR-038 — live code ("config parse error") renumbered to E-CFG-104.** `org_slug` field value does not case-sensitively match the filename stem. R-CUST-002. |

NOTE: Collision resolved per ADR-038 — live code ("config validation failed") renumbered to E-CFG-102.** `org_id` parses as a valid UUID but version nibble ≠ 7. R-CUST-003. |
| E-CFG-004 | broken | configuration | "customers/{file}: E-CFG-004: unknown DTU type '{type}'" | No | **RETIRED per ADR-037 (2026-06-10); rule R-CUST-004 preserved in BC-3.3.004 body. R-CUST-004. |

R-CUST-010. |

| E-CFG-012 | broken | configuration | "E-CFG-012: org_slug '{slug}' declared in both '{file1}' and '{file2}'" | No | **RETIRED per ADR-037 (2026-06-10); rule R-CUST-012 preserved in BC-3.3.004 body. R-CUST-012. |

No successor code — production customer config no longer declares DTU types (surface retired with the ADR-010 schema).** `[[dtu]] type` is in `DTU_DEFAULT_MODE` but has `test_only = true` annotation (e.g., `demo-server`). Distinct from E-CFG-004: the type IS in the registry but is production-forbidden. R-CUST-013. |
| E-CFG-014 | broken | configuration | "customers/{file}: E-CFG-014: [[dtu]] type '{type}' has mode='client' but 'spec' field is missing; provide a path to the sensor spec TOML" | No | **RETIRED per ADR-037 (2026-06-10); rule R-CUST-014 preserved in BC-3.3.004 body. No successor code — the `spec` field is retired; per-org sensor config is the ADR-029 overlay directory, where an absent overlay means TYPE defaults apply (BC-2.06.012).** `[[dtu]]` block has `mode = "client"` but the `spec` field is absent. R-CUST-014. |
| E-CFG-015 | broken | configuration | "customers/{file}: E-CFG-015: spec path '{path}' does not exist on disk" | No | **RETIRED per ADR-037 (2026-06-10); rule R-CUST-015 preserved in BC-3.3.004 body. Nearest successor analogue: E-SPEC-019 (overlay `extends` references no loaded TYPE spec) per BC-2.06.012/BC-2.06.016 — config-declared file paths are retired; overlay discovery enumerates the directory.** `[[dtu]]` block has `mode = "client"` and a `spec` field is present, but the referenced TOML file does not exist on disk. File existence is checked in the validation pass (ADR-010 D-053) to preserve the zero-partial-registration invariant. R-CUST-015. |
| E-CFG-016 | broken | configuration | "customers/{file}: E-CFG-016: [[dtu]] type '{type}' has mode='shared' but 'spec' field is present; 'spec' is only valid for mode='client'" | No | **RETIRED per ADR-037 (2026-06-10); rule R-CUST-016 preserved in BC-3.3.004 body. No successor code — the `mode` and `spec` fields are retired with the ADR-010 schema.** `[[dtu]]` block has `mode = "shared"` and the `spec` field is present. `spec` is a known schema field, so `deny_unknown_fields` does not catch this — requires explicit semantic validation (ADR-010 §2.3 rule 5). R-CUST-016. |
| E-CFG-017 | broken | configuration | "customers/{file}: E-CFG-017: DTU type '{type}' is a Security Telemetry type and must be configured with mode='client'; mode='shared' would cause cross-tenant data leakage" | No | **RETIRED per ADR-037 (2026-06-10); rule table preserved in BC-3.3.001 body. No successor code — cross-tenant ST isolation is structural on the superseding surface: compile-time `DtuMode` (S-3.0.02), per-org instance binding (BC-2.06.017), per-org seeded data (BC-2.06.018), deployment-time-only mode (BC-3.2.005).** `[[dtu]]` block declares a Security Telemetry type (claroty, armis, crowdstrike, cyberint, demo-server) with `mode = "shared"`. The guard is unconditional in Wave 3 (no `allow_shared_override`). BC-3.3.001. |
| E-CFG-018 | broken | configuration | "customers/{file}: E-CFG-018: spec path '{path}' traverses outside the allowed directory; paths must remain within the prism config root" | No | **RETIRED per ADR-037 (2026-06-10); rule preserved in BC-3.3.001/BC-3.3.004 bodies. No successor code — operator-supplied spec paths are retired; ADR-029 overlay paths derive from directory enumeration of `customers/<org_slug>/`, not from config-declared path values, so the CWE-22 vector is structurally absent on the successor surface.** `[[dtu]]` block has `mode = "client"` and a `spec` field whose canonicalized path escapes the config root boundary (CWE-22 path traversal). Introduced by W3-FIX-SEC-003 (PR #114, a68d1748). Mitigation: `validate_spec_path` canonicalizes the resolved path and rejects any path whose prefix does not begin with the config root. Severity: HIGH. Used in: prism-customer-config validator. BC-3.3.001/BC-3.3.004. |
| E-CFG-019 | broken | configuration | "customers/{file}: E-CFG-019: org_slug '{slug}' does not match required pattern ^[a-zA-Z0-9_-]{{1,64}}$" | No | **RETIRED per ADR-037 (2026-06-10); rule preserved in BC-3.3.001 body. Successor surface: kebab-case org_slug check on `prism.toml` `[[orgs]]` per BC-2.21.001 (boot step 3; exit 2; no E- code); the `OrgSlug` newtype pattern validation itself survives in prism-core.** `org_slug` field value fails the `OrgSlug` regex validation (CWE-20 improper input validation). Introduced by W3-FIX-CODE-002 (PR #120, a7f0d374). Mitigation: `OrgSlug` regex `^[a-zA-Z0-9_-]{1,64}$` applied at parse time. Severity: MEDIUM. Used in: prism-customer-config validator. BC-3.3.001. |

### CFG-000, CFG-020, CFG-030, CFG-031: Additional Customer Config Validation Errors (Wave 3)

> **SECTION RETIRED per ADR-037 (2026-06-10).** Same retirement as the CFG-001..014 section above: all four codes were emitted only by the retired `prism-customer-config` crate against the retired ADR-010 schema. IDs preserved per append_only_numbering (DF-030). Rule tables preserved in the retired BC bodies: E-CFG-000/030/031 in BC-3.3.003; E-CFG-020 (R-CRED-001..006) in BC-3.3.002. See also the namespace-collision drift note above — adjudicated per ADR-038: the live `prism_core::PrismError` "invalid capability path" semantic that formerly emitted E-CFG-020 is renumbered to E-CFG-106 and cataloged in the CFG-100..106 runtime section.

These codes covered TOML type errors, literal credential detection, and schema version errors. All were emitted during the `customers/*.toml` startup validation pass.

| Code | Severity | Category | Message Format | Retryable | Description |
|------|----------|----------|---------------|-----------|-------------|
| E-CFG-000 | broken | configuration | "customers/{file}: E-CFG-000: TOML parse error: {serde_error}" | No | **RETIRED per ADR-037 (2026-06-10); rule preserved in BC-3.3.003 body (EC-3.3.003-06/07). No successor code — the `customers/{org_slug}.toml` schema is retired; parse validation of the successor config surfaces is owned by their own contracts (BC-2.21.001 for `prism.toml`; BC-2.06.012–016 for ADR-029 overlays).** TOML structural or type error (e.g., `schema_version = "1"` when an integer is required). Emitted when `serde` deserialization fails due to a wrong field type. The `{serde_error}` field contains the serde-generated message (e.g., "expected integer for schema_version, got string"). BC-3.3.003 EC-3.3.003-06/07. |
| E-CFG-020 | broken | configuration | "customers/{file}: E-CFG-020: field '{name}' appears to contain a literal credential; use a credential_ref with an opaque reference scheme (vault://, env://, file://, keyring://) instead" | No | **RETIRED per ADR-037 (2026-06-10); rule table (R-CRED-001..006) preserved in BC-3.3.002 body. Successor: structural — the ADR-032/BC-2.06.003 reference-based credential model means config files carry only `[[credential_refs]]` names (validated per DI-014; invalid names emit successor code E-CRED-001); literal credential values are no longer expressible on the config surface. NOTE: Collision resolved per ADR-038 — live code ("invalid capability path") renumbered to E-CFG-106.** A field whose name matches a credential-like pattern (ends in `_token`, `_secret`, `_key`, `_password`, `_pass`, or is exactly `password`) contains a non-empty string that does not begin with one of the four allowed opaque reference scheme prefixes. The field value is NEVER echoed in the error message to prevent credential exposure in logs. R-CRED-001..006 in BC-3.3.002. |
| E-CFG-030 | broken | configuration | "customers/{file}: E-CFG-030: missing required field 'schema_version'" | No | **RETIRED per ADR-037 (2026-06-10); rule preserved in BC-3.3.003 body. No successor code — `schema_version` is exclusive to the retired ADR-010 schema (`replacement: null` per BC-3.3.003; ADR-037 §Consequences accepts no successor).** The `schema_version` field is entirely absent from the top-level TOML document. Distinct from E-CFG-031 (field present but unsupported value) and E-CFG-001 (other required fields absent). BC-3.3.003. |
| E-CFG-031 | broken | configuration | "customers/{file}: E-CFG-031: schema_version {value} is not supported; only schema_version 1 is supported in this binary[. Run 'prism config migrate customers/' to upgrade.]" | No | **RETIRED per ADR-037 (2026-06-10); rule preserved in BC-3.3.003 body. No successor code — `schema_version` is exclusive to the retired ADR-010 schema (`replacement: null` per BC-3.3.003; ADR-037 §Consequences accepts no successor). The `prism config migrate` hint is retired with the code.** The `schema_version` field is present but its integer value is not equal to `1`. The migration hint ("Run 'prism config migrate customers/' to upgrade.") is appended only when `schema_version > 1` (future schema); it is omitted for `schema_version < 1`. BC-3.3.003. |

### CFG-100..106: Runtime Config Errors

These are the live runtime config codes emitted by `prism_core::PrismError` (plus spec-declared E-CFG-101); renumbered from E-CFG-001..004 in v1.8; extended to E-CFG-104..106 per ADR-038 (2026-06-10).

> **Tombstone permanence (ADR-038 D5):** the retired customer-config numbers E-CFG-000 through E-CFG-031 are permanently tombstoned per POL-1 append_only_numbering (DF-030) and are never reusable for any future condition. Future runtime extension allocates **E-CFG-107 onward**; the 000..031 range is closed forever.

| Code | Severity | Category | Message Format | Retryable | Description |
|------|----------|----------|---------------|-----------|-------------|
| E-CFG-100 | broken | configuration | "E-CFG-100: client '{client_id}' not found in configuration" | No | Referenced client not configured (tool param or alias scope names an unknown client). Emitted by `PrismError::ClientNotFound` (ADR-038 D3 variant split). Formerly E-CFG-001 (pre-v1.8). Used in: prism-query alias tools, prism-security list_capabilities. BC-2.10.004, BC-2.11.001/008/011/013/014, BC-2.08.008, BC-2.14.010. |
| E-CFG-101 | broken | configuration | "E-CFG-101: missing required field: {toml_path}" | No | Required field missing from runtime config TOML (formerly E-CFG-002 pre-v1.8). Spec-declared; no `PrismError` variant yet — forward-declared for the AD-007 hot-reload surface (ADR-038). |
| E-CFG-102 | broken | configuration | "E-CFG-102: config validation failed: {detail}" | No | Runtime config type/value/semantic validation failure (formerly E-CFG-003 pre-v1.8). `{detail}` SHOULD carry the toml_path, expected, and actual values. Emitted by `PrismError::ConfigValidationFailed` (renumbered from code-side E-CFG-003 per ADR-038 D2). |
| E-CFG-103 | broken | configuration | "E-CFG-103: config file not found: {path}" | No | Config file does not exist at the resolved path (formerly E-CFG-004 pre-v1.8). Emitted by `PrismError::ConfigNotFound` (renumbered from code-side E-CFG-001 per ADR-038 D2). |
| E-CFG-104 | broken | configuration | "E-CFG-104: config parse error: {detail}" | No | TOML structural/parse error on a runtime config surface (AD-007 hot-reload; boot-time parse failures use the BC-2.21.001 exit-2 path with no E- code). Emitted by `PrismError::ConfigParseFailed` (renumbered from uncataloged code-side E-CFG-002 per ADR-038 D2). |
| E-CFG-105 | broken | transient | "E-CFG-105: config snapshot stale: version {current} < required {required}" | Yes | ArcSwap config snapshot version below required version (AD-007). Retry acquires a fresh `ArcSwap::load()` snapshot. Emitted by `PrismError::ConfigSnapshotStale` (renumbered from uncataloged code-side E-CFG-010 per ADR-038 D2). |
| E-CFG-106 | broken | validation | "E-CFG-106: invalid capability path: {reason}" | No | Capability path string fails `CapabilityPath::new()` format rules: empty string, empty segment, invalid characters, more than 8 segments, or total length > 256 chars. Emitted by `PrismError::InvalidCapabilityPath` (renumbered from uncataloged code-side E-CFG-020 per ADR-038 D2). Used in: prism-core capability, prism-security. |

| E-MCP-002 | broken | permission | "Tool '{name}' not available for client '{client_id}'" | No | Tool hidden by feature flags, agent somehow invoked it |

## AUDIT: Audit Errors

| Code | Severity | Category | Message Format | Retryable | Description |
|------|----------|----------|---------------|-----------|-------------|
| E-AUDIT-001 | broken | transient | "Audit emission failed; write operation blocked" | Yes | Audit subscriber failed during a write operation; the write was not executed. Retry may succeed if the subscriber recovers. |
| E-AUDIT-002 | degraded | transient | "Vector endpoint unreachable for audit log forwarding" | Yes | External audit destination unavailable; entries accumulate in RocksDB buffer with exponential backoff retry |
| E-AUDIT-003 | degraded | transient | "Audit buffer approaching capacity ({count}/{max} entries)" | No | Buffer nearing 100K limit; oldest entries will be purged if limit exceeded |
| E-AUDIT-004 | broken | transient | "Audit buffer purge operation failed: {reason}" | Yes | RocksDB error during overflow purge; buffer continues growing; next purge cycle retries |
| E-AUDIT-005 | degraded | configuration | "Audit forward permanent failure for destination '{name}': {reason}. Entries retained in buffer; fix config and reload." | No | Destination permanently unreachable (HTTP 4xx, DNS failure). Watermark does not advance. Entry preserved. Added by BC-2.05.011. |
| E-AUDIT-006 | broken | transient | "Audit buffer capacity exceeded: evicting {count} entries. At-least-once guarantee LOST for evicted entries. Check audit forward destination health." | No | FIFO eviction occurred because audit_buffer reached the 100K cap and a lagging destination prevented GC. CRITICAL severity — emitted as CRITICAL log in addition to structured error. Added by BC-2.05.011. |

## QUERY: Query Engine Errors

| Code | Severity | Category | Message Format | Retryable | Description |
|------|----------|----------|---------------|-----------|-------------|
| E-QUERY-001 | broken | validation | `"E-QUERY-001: parse error{location}: {message}"` where `{location}` is `" near '{token}'"` when a specific error-site token is available (mode-bridge errors: `" near '|'"` or `" near 'SELECT'"`) or `" at position {pos}"` for position-based errors | No | PrismQL query string cannot be parsed (syntax error, unknown keyword, unknown field, or mode-mixing error). **Mode-bridge variant (ADR-046 D1 — `\|` in SQL mode):** when SQL-mode parse fails at a `\|` token that is not a valid SQL→Pipe composition (BC-2.11.020), the generic Chumsky expectation list is replaced with a structured mode-bridge message naming the correct pipe or composed form. **Mode-bridge variant (ADR-046 D2 — SQL clause in pipe position):** when `SELECT` or `ORDER BY` appears in pipe stage position, the error message names the correct lowercase stage keywords. **`normalized_pql` field:** `StructuredErrorFields` carries `normalized_pql: Option<String>` — a best-effort pipe-mode rewrite of the failing query, present on mode-bridge errors when a simple `FROM/WHERE/LIMIT` rewrite is unambiguous; `null` otherwise. (Note: there is NO `ParseErrorDetails` type; D-1110 established that `normalized_pql` lives on `StructuredErrorFields` — BC-2.11.023 §D1 was corrected in v1.2.) This field is an additive non-breaking extension to the structured error response (BC-2.11.023, ADR-046 D3). MCP mapping: `-32602 INVALID_PARAMS` (caller-resolvable). **BC anchors:** BC-2.11.002 (filter mode), BC-2.11.003 (SQL mode), BC-2.11.004 (pipe mode), BC-2.11.023 (mode-bridge diagnostics + `normalized_pql`), BC-2.11.024 (SQL-mode rejection of IEQ/IIN/INE — mode-boundary enforcement; parse-time trap closure for operators that are filter/pipe-only). |
| E-QUERY-002 | broken | validation | **COLLIDED — two Display formats:** (a) `PrismError::QueryPlanFailed` (pre-existing): `"E-QUERY-002: query planning failed: {detail}"` — generic query plan construction failure; (b) `PrismError::QueryTypeMismatch` (ADR-041 L4, S-DEMO-PRISMQL-ONBOARDING-001-B): two sub-forms depending on whether a string-column sibling is known — (b1) with suggestion: `"E-QUERY-002: type mismatch — column '{column}' in table '{table}' has type '{actual_type:?}' which does not support operator '{operator}'; for label comparison, use the string column '{suggested_column}' with IEQ/IIN/INE instead"` — (b2) without suggestion: `"E-QUERY-002: type mismatch — column '{column}' in table '{table}' has type '{actual_type:?}' which does not support operator '{operator}'"` — plan-time type-compatibility gate (BC-2.11.017) | No | **LIVE CODE COLLISION (v1.92 finding):** Two semantically distinct conditions share this code. (a) `PrismError::QueryPlanFailed { detail: String }` — generic query-planning failure (query cannot be planned for reasons other than type mismatch). MCP maps to `-32000 INTERNAL_ERROR`. Pre-existing variant, develop branch. (b) `PrismError::QueryTypeMismatch { column, table, actual_type, operator, suggested_column: Option<String> }` — plan-time type-compatibility gate: the model used an operator that is not valid for the column's declared `ColumnType`. Introduced by S-DEMO-PRISMQL-ONBOARDING-001-B (ADR-041 L4); amended in S-PRISMQL-CASE-INSENSITIVE-001 pass-2 to add `suggested_column` field (v2.18). MCP maps to `-32602 INVALID_PARAMS` (caller-resolvable: switch to a valid operator or use the suggested string column). The structured error response for (b) includes the `valid_operators_for_type` field per BC-2.11.017 §E-QUERY-002 — this machine-readable array is the canonical contract; the Display is human/LLM-facing prose. **`suggested_column` population:** the caller constructing `QueryTypeMismatch` MUST perform an OCSF sibling lookup to populate `suggested_column`; the mapping is: `"severity_id"` → `Some("severity")`; `"status_id"` → `Some("status")`; `"activity_id"` → `Some("activity_name")` (OCSF sibling exception — see BC-2.02.013 v1.2); `"disposition_id"` → `Some("disposition")`; any other `_id`-suffixed or non-id column → `None`. When `suggested_column = None`, the Display uses sub-form (b2). This enrichment is NOT a third trigger condition — it amends the existing `QueryTypeMismatch` variant's struct fields and Display; the prohibition on a third condition (PrismError variant emitting E-QUERY-002) remains in force. **Adjudication (S-PRISMQL-CASE-INSENSITIVE-001 pass-2):** "no third condition" governs PrismError VARIANT TRIGGERS, not message content; enriching an existing variant's Display with an agent-helpful suggestion is permitted and preferred per the agent-harness design principle (project memory `project_agent_harness_design.md`). **Pre-collision taxonomy row** `"Type error: field '{field}' is {actual_type}, cannot use {operator}"` matched neither live Display format and has been superseded by this collision-documenting row (v1.92). **Blast-radius renumbering to separate (a) and (b) is deferred** to a future maintenance story (analogous to E-AUTH-001/002 collision documented in §AUTH). DO NOT add a third condition that emits E-QUERY-002. BC anchor for (b): BC-2.11.017 §E-QUERY-002. |
| E-QUERY-003 | broken | validation | `"E-QUERY-003: {limit_detail}"` | No | Query exceeds a syntactic security limit enforced by the parser security pre-/post-scan (BC-2.11.006, DI-019): query size > 64KB, expression/paren nesting depth > 64, unclosed string literal at end of input (quote-scan integrity guard), list item count, pipe stage count > 32, regex pattern length > 1024 bytes, alias-expanded query size > 64KB (BC-2.11.009; BC-2.11.010 explain path). `{limit_detail}` is the per-limit message family, e.g. "query size {n} bytes exceeds maximum allowed {max} bytes (64KB limit)", "expression nesting depth {depth} exceeds maximum allowed {limit}", "pipe stage count {n} exceeds maximum allowed {max}", "regex pattern length {n} bytes exceeds maximum allowed {max} bytes", "expanded query exceeds 64KB limit ({n} bytes)". **SECURITY LIMITS ONLY** — emitted by `PrismError::QuerySecurityLimitExceeded { detail }` (dedicated variant per QRY cascade P5-02 adjudication 2026-06-10, ADR-038 v1.3). Pre-split the condition routed through `QueryExecutionFailed` with an embedded prefix, producing the double-prefixed display "E-QUERY-003: query execution error: E-QUERY-003: query size..." (security.rs `check_query_size` family); un-nesting eliminates it. Verified by VP-014 (size) and VP-015 (depth) Kani proofs — property text pins the `E-QUERY-003` substring and is unchanged by the split. Surfaces through `map_prism_error` as MCP `-32602` INVALID_PARAMS with the display string (caller-resolvable: narrow/simplify the query; the pre-split opaque `-32000` mapping contradicted BC-2.11.006's structured caller-visible responses). Distinct from E-QUERY-033 (tool-level `limit` parameter ceiling) and E-QUERY-034 (generic execution error). |
| E-QUERY-004 | degraded | transient | `"E-QUERY-004: query timed out after {elapsed_ms}ms"` | Yes | Query execution exceeded the 30s timeout. Retryable with a narrower scope. Use `force_execute: true` to override. |

| E-QUERY-010 | broken | validation | "Internal tables are read-only via PrismQL. Use the dedicated MCP tool: {tool_name}" | No | SQL write statement (INSERT/UPDATE/DELETE) targets an internal Prism table; mutations go through dedicated MCP tools |

Grant via prism.toml [clients.{id}.capabilities]."` | No | Query targets `prism_audit` (or any internal table whose descriptor requires audit-read) but the caller lacks the `audit.read` capability. Emitted by `PrismError::AuditTableAccessDenied` (prism-core `error.rs`, fieldless variant); raised by the engine's descriptor-driven `check_table_access` gate (`internal_tables::table_requires_audit_read`) after recursive source-name extraction — the denial applies to the table in FROM, subquery, and HAVING positions alike. Message Format is the verbatim shipped display (ADR-035 canonical-row convention); the display intentionally contains the substring "audit.read capability" for caller substring detection. Surfaces through `map_prism_error` as MCP `-32002` FORBIDDEN with the display string (caller-resolvable: grant `audit.read = "Allow"` under `[clients.{id}.capabilities]`). BC anchor: BC-2.15.011 (capability gate + error table + EC-15-013); story S-2.03 (merged). History: code shipped for this condition by S-2.03 while the taxonomy's only 011 row still described the never-implemented "table removed after config reload" condition (BC-2.16.007) — a two-BC code collision; the reload condition re-homed to E-QUERY-035 per MCP cascade P4-05 adjudication 2026-06-10 (ADR-038 v1.4 — D5 family, fourth instance this cycle). |
